Because binary spectra overestimate absolute latent dimensionality, we interpret ED as a screening statistic rather than a literal factor count and complement it with null, reliability, and saturation analyses. We provide a 22-benchmark reference atlas and a four-step diagnostic workflow that benchmark maintainers can run with a score matrix and a few lines of code.

</details>


### [150] [Rigorous Explanations for Tree Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29361)
*Yacine Izza,Alexey Ignatiev,Xuanxiang Huang,Peter J. Stuckey,Joao Marques-Silva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何为树集成模型（随机森林和提升树）计算严格定义、逻辑上可靠的解释，以建立对这些黑盒模型预测的信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树集成模型虽然准确且应用广泛，但其决策过程对人类决策者来说是不透明的。为了建立对这些模型预测的信任，需要自动生成解释。只有当这些解释是严格的、真正反映底层预测器属性时，才能实现信任。

Method: 研究为两种树集成模型（随机森林和提升树）计算严格定义、逻辑上可靠的解释方法。这些解释基于逻辑推理，确保其严谨性和可靠性。

Result: 论文提出了为树集成模型生成逻辑上可靠解释的计算方法，这些解释能够严格反映模型的预测机制，从而增强模型的可解释性和可信度。

Conclusion: 通过为树集成模型提供严格定义、逻辑上可靠的解释，可以解决这些模型的不透明性问题，建立用户对其预测的信任，这对于树集成模型在实际应用中的可信部署至关重要。

Abstract: Tree ensembles (TEs) find a multitude of practical applications. They represent one of the most general and accurate classes of machine learning methods. While they are typically quite concise in representation, their operation remains inscrutable to human decision makers. One solution to build trust in the operation of TEs is to automatically identify explanations for the predictions made. Evidently, we can only achieve trust using explanations, if those explanations are rigorous, that is truly reflect properties of the underlying predictor they explain This paper investigates the computation of rigorously-defined, logically-sound explanations for the concrete case of two well-known examples of tree ensembles, namely random forests and boosted trees.

</details>


### [151] [AI-Generated Prior Authorization Letters: Strong Clinical Content, Weak Administrative Scaffolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29366)
*Moiz Sadiq Awan,Maryam Raza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs能生成临床内容准确的预授权信函，但缺乏行政精确性（如计费代码、授权期限等），实际部署挑战在于系统能否提供行政精确性而非临床能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预授权是美国医疗中最繁重的行政流程之一，消耗大量资源和时间。虽然LLM在临床文本任务中表现出潜力，但其生成提交就绪的预授权信函的能力尚未得到充分评估，现有研究仅限于单案例演示而非结构化多场景评估。

Method: 评估了三种商用LLM（GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.5和Gemini 2.5 Pro），使用45个经过医生验证的合成场景，涵盖风湿病学、精神病学、肿瘤学、心脏病学和骨科。除了临床内容评估外，还进行了真实世界行政要求的二次分析。

Result: 所有三种模型生成的预授权信函都具有强大的临床内容：准确的诊断、结构良好的医疗必要性论证以及全面的阶梯治疗记录。然而，对真实世界行政要求的分析揭示了临床评分未能捕捉的一致缺陷，包括缺失的计费代码、缺少的授权期限请求以及不充分的随访计划。

Conclusion: 临床部署的挑战不在于LLM能否撰写临床充分的信函，而在于围绕它们构建的系统能否提供支付方工作流程所需的行政精确性。研究重新定义了问题焦点：需要关注行政精确性而不仅仅是临床能力。

Abstract: Prior authorization remains one of the most burdensome administrative processes in U.S. healthcare, consuming billions of dollars and thousands of physician hours each year. While large language models have shown promise across clinical text tasks, their ability to produce submission-ready prior authorization letters has received only limited attention, with existing work confined to single-case demonstrations rather than structured multi-scenario evaluation. We assessed three commercially available LLMs (GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4.5, and Gemini 2.5 Pro) across 45 physician-validated synthetic scenarios spanning rheumatology, psychiatry, oncology, cardiology, and orthopedics. All three models generated letters with strong clinical content: accurate diagnoses, well-structured medical necessity arguments, and thorough step therapy documentation. However, a secondary analysis of real-world administrative requirements revealed consistent gaps that clinical scoring alone did not capture, including absent billing codes, missing authorization duration requests, and inadequate follow-up plans. These findings reframe the question: the challenge for clinical deployment is not whether LLMs can write clinically adequate letters, but whether the systems built around them can supply the administrative precision that payer workflows require.

</details>


### [152] [Structural Compactness as a Complementary Criterion for Explanation Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29491)
*Mohammad Mahdi Mesgari,Jackie Ma,Wojciech Samek,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Leander Weber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MST-C指标，基于最小生成树评估归因解释的紧凑性，衡量归因点的空间分布与聚类结构


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定量评估方法难以捕捉归因解释的可读性，因为归因的形状和内部组织差异无法通过简单统计量衡量

Method: 引入最小生成树紧凑度(MST-C)，基于图的结构化指标，捕捉归因的高阶几何特性（如扩散度和内聚性），将多个组件组合为单一评分

Result: MST-C能可靠区分不同解释方法，揭示模型间的根本结构差异，提供稳健的自包含诊断指标，补充现有归因复杂性概念

Conclusion: MST-C作为评估归因解释紧凑性的有效指标，能够捕捉传统统计方法无法衡量的结构特性，为解释质量评估提供新维度

Abstract: In the evaluation of attribution quality, the quantitative assessment of explanation legibility is particularly difficult, as it is influenced by varying shapes and internal organization of attributions not captured by simple statistics. To address this issue, we introduce Minimum Spanning Tree Compactness (MST-C), a graph-based structural metric that captures higher-order geometric properties of attributions, such as spread and cohesion. These components are combined into a single score that evaluates compactness, favoring attributions with salient points spread across a small area and spatially organized into few but cohesive clusters. We show that MST-C reliably distinguishes between explanation methods, exposes fundamental structural differences between models, and provides a robust, self-contained diagnostic for explanation compactness that complements existing notions of attribution complexity.

</details>


### [153] [Learning to Generate Formally Verifiable Step-by-Step Logic Reasoning via Structured Formal Intermediaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29500)
*Luoxin Chen,Yichi Zhou,Huishuai Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRoSFI是一种通过结构化中间步骤的形式验证来增强LLM推理可靠性的奖励方法，在保持准确性的同时提高推理可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于结果奖励的强化学习方法虽然能提高LLM在复杂多步推理任务上的表现，但往往忽略有缺陷的中间步骤，导致即使最终答案正确，推理过程也不可靠。

Method: 提出PRoSFI方法：模型输出与自然语言推理对齐的结构化中间步骤，每个步骤由形式验证器验证，只有完全验证通过的推理链才能获得高奖励，从而引导模型生成可机器验证的逐步证明。

Result: 该方法能够在不损害准确性的前提下增强推理可靠性，为训练可信推理模型提供了一种简单有效的途径。

Conclusion: PRoSFI通过结合形式验证的结构化中间步骤奖励机制，有效解决了LLM推理过程中中间步骤不可靠的问题，提升了推理模型的可信度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated impressive performance on complex, multi-step reasoning tasks, especially when post-trained with outcome-rewarded reinforcement learning Guo et al. 2025. However, it has been observed that outcome rewards often overlook flawed intermediate steps, leading to unreliable reasoning steps even when final answers are correct. To address this unreliable reasoning, we propose PRoSFI (Process Reward over Structured Formal Intermediates), a novel reward method that enhances reasoning reliability without compromising accuracy. Instead of generating formal proofs directly, which is rarely accomplishable for a modest-sized (7B) model, the model outputs structured intermediate steps aligned with its natural language reasoning. Each step is then verified by a formal prover. Only fully validated reasoning chains receive high rewards. The integration of formal verification guides the model towards generating step-by-step machine-checkable proofs, thereby yielding more credible final answers. PRoSFI offers a simple and effective approach to training trustworthy reasoning models.

</details>


### [154] [FlowPIE: Test-Time Scientific Idea Evolution with Flow-Guided Literature Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29557)
*Qiyao Wang,Hongbo Wang,Longze Chen,Zhihao Yang,Guhong Chen,Hamid Alinejad-Rokny,Hui Li,Yuan Lin,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowPIE是一个紧密耦合的检索-生成框架，将文献探索和科学想法生成建模为协同进化过程，通过流引导的MCTS和基于LLM的生成奖励模型来产生多样且高质量的科学想法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI驱动的自主研究中，科学想法生成方法通常受限于静态的"检索-然后-生成"范式，导致想法同质化且缺乏发散性，无法有效突破信息茧房。

Method: 1) 使用流引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索（受GFlowNets启发）扩展文献轨迹，以LLM生成的奖励模型评估当前想法质量作为监督信号；2) 基于初始种群，将想法生成建模为测试时进化过程，应用选择、交叉、变异操作，采用隔离岛范式和基于GRM的适应度计算来整合跨领域知识。

Result: FlowPIE相比现有的基于LLM和基于智能体的框架，能够持续产生具有更高新颖性、可行性和多样性的科学想法，同时在测试时实现奖励缩放。

Conclusion: FlowPIE通过将文献探索和想法生成紧密耦合为协同进化过程，有效缓解了过度依赖参数化知识和静态文献导致的信息茧房问题，为AI驱动的自主研究提供了更有效的科学想法生成框架。

Abstract: Scientific idea generation (SIG) is critical to AI-driven autonomous research, yet existing approaches are often constrained by a static retrieval-then-generation paradigm, leading to homogeneous and insufficiently divergent ideas. In this work, we propose FlowPIE, a tightly coupled retrieval-generation framework that treats literature exploration and idea generation as a co-evolving process. FlowPIE expands literature trajectories via a flow-guided Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) inspired by GFlowNets, using the quality of current ideas assessed by an LLM-based generative reward model (GRM) as a supervised signal to guide adaptive retrieval and construct a diverse, high-quality initial population. Based on this population, FlowPIE models idea generation as a test-time idea evolution process, applying selection, crossover, and mutation with the isolation island paradigm and GRM-based fitness computation to incorporate cross-domain knowledge. It effectively mitigates the information cocoons arising from over-reliance on parametric knowledge and static literature. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that FlowPIE consistently produces ideas with higher novelty, feasibility and diversity compared to strong LLM-based and agent-based frameworks, while enabling reward scaling during test time.

</details>


### [155] [Beyond the Steeper Curve: AI-Mediated Metacognitive Decoupling and the Limits of the Dunning-Kruger Metaphor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29681)
*Christopher Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文挑战了"生成式AI单纯放大邓宁-克鲁格效应"的普遍观点，提出AI介导的元认知解耦模型，解释了AI使用如何同时改善任务表现却损害元认知准确性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于生成式AI与邓宁-克鲁格效应的关系描述过于粗糙，无法解释复杂的实证证据。需要更精细的理论框架来解释AI使用如何同时改善任务表现却损害元认知准确性这一矛盾现象

Method: 综合人类-AI交互、学习研究和模型评估的证据，提出AI介导的元认知解耦工作模型，该模型包含四个变量：产出输出、底层理解、校准准确性和自我评估能力

Result: AI使用能改善可观察输出和短期任务表现，但会降低元认知准确性，并削弱不同技能群体间的能力-信心梯度。四变量模型比简单的"更陡峭的邓宁-克鲁格曲线"隐喻能更好地解释过度自信、过度/不足依赖、拐杖效应和弱迁移等现象

Conclusion: 需要超越简单的邓宁-克鲁格效应放大论，采用更精细的AI介导元认知解耦模型。这对工具设计、评估和知识工作具有重要启示，需要关注如何平衡AI带来的生产力提升与元认知能力保持

Abstract: The common claim that generative AI simply amplifies the Dunning-Kruger effect is too coarse to capture the available evidence. The clearest findings instead suggest that large language model (LLM) use can improve observable output and short-term task performance while degrading metacognitive accuracy and flattening the classic competence-confidence gradient across skill groups. This paper synthesizes evidence from human-AI interaction, learning research, and model evaluation, and proposes the working model of AI-mediated metacognitive decoupling: a widening gap among produced output, underlying understanding, calibration accuracy, and self-assessed ability. This four-variable account better explains overconfidence, over- and under-reliance, crutch effects, and weak transfer than the simpler metaphor of a uniformly steeper Dunning-Kruger curve. The paper concludes with implications for tool design, assessment, and knowledge work.

</details>


### [156] [A First Step Towards Even More Sparse Encodings of Probability Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29691)
*Florian Andreas Marwitz,Tanya Braun,Ralf Möller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出从概率分布中提取一阶逻辑公式的方法，通过减少分布中的数值数量并提取最小化逻辑公式，实现编码稀疏化和分布泛化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界场景可以用提升概率分布来捕捉，但传统分布通常以表格或列表形式编码，需要指数级数量的值，导致表示效率低下

Method: 首先减少分布中的数值数量，然后为每个值提取逻辑公式并进行最小化，通过这种约简和最小化过程实现编码稀疏化和分布泛化

Result: 评估显示，通过提取少量简短公式可以极大增加编码稀疏度，同时保留核心信息

Conclusion: 该方法能够从概率分布中高效提取一阶逻辑公式，显著减少所需数值数量，实现更稀疏的编码表示

Abstract: Real world scenarios can be captured with lifted probability distributions. However, distributions are usually encoded in a table or list, requiring an exponential number of values. Hence, we propose a method for extracting first-order formulas from probability distributions that require significantly less values by reducing the number of values in a distribution and then extracting, for each value, a logical formula to be further minimized. This reduction and minimization allows for increasing the sparsity in the encoding while also generalizing a given distribution. Our evaluation shows that sparsity can increase immensely by extracting a small set of short formulas while preserving core information.

</details>


### [157] [Measuring the metacognition of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29693)
*Richard Servajean,Philippe Servajean*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用meta-d'框架及其无模型替代方案作为评估AI元认知敏感性的黄金标准，并利用信号检测理论测量AI基于不确定性和风险自发调节决策的能力，通过两个系列实验在三个大语言模型上验证了这些方法的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地集成到决策工作流中，管理不确定性越来越依赖于这些系统的元认知能力（即评估自身决策可靠性并调节决策的能力）。因此，需要采用稳健的方法来测量AI的元认知能力，以确保AI系统在风险决策中的可靠性。

Method: 提出采用meta-d'框架或其无模型替代方案作为评估AI元认知敏感性的黄金标准；利用信号检测理论测量AI基于不确定性和风险自发调节决策的能力。通过两个系列实验验证：1）LLMs执行主要判断后给出置信度评分；2）LLMs仅执行主要判断，同时操纵与响应相关的风险。

Result: 应用meta-d'框架可以在三个维度进行比较：将LLM与最优性比较、不同LLM在相同任务上的比较、同一LLM在不同任务上的比较。信号检测理论分析表明，当风险较高时，LLMs会变得更加保守。

Conclusion: meta-d'框架和信号检测理论为评估AI系统的元认知能力提供了有效的心理物理学框架，这些方法能够量化AI在不确定性和风险环境下的决策质量，对于确保AI系统在关键应用中的可靠性具有重要意义。

Abstract: A robust decision-making process must take into account uncertainty, especially when the choice involves inherent risks. Because artificial Intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly integrated into decision-making workflows, managing uncertainty relies more and more on the metacognitive capabilities of these systems; i.e, their ability to assess the reliability of and regulate their own decisions. Hence, it is crucial to employ robust methods to measure the metacognitive abilities of AI. This paper is primarily a methodological contribution arguing for the adoption of the meta-d' framework, or its model-free alternatives, as the gold standard for assessing the metacognitive sensitivity of AIs--the ability to generate confidence ratings that distinguish correct from incorrect responses. Moreover, we propose to leverage signal detection theory (SDT) to measure the ability of AIs to spontaneously regulate their decisions based on uncertainty and risk. To demonstrate the practical utility of these psychophysical frameworks, we conduct two series of experiments on three large language models (LLMs)--GPT-5, DeepSeek-V3.2-Exp, and Mistral-Medium-2508. In the first experiments, LLMs performed a primary judgment followed by a confidence rating. In the second, LLMs only performed the primary judgment, while we manipulated the risk associated with either response. On the one hand, applying the meta-d' framework allows us to conduct comparisons along three axes: comparing an LLM to optimality, comparing different LLMs on a given task, and comparing the same LLM across different tasks. On the other hand, SDT allows us to assess whether LLMs become more conservative when risks are high.

</details>


### [158] [Symphony for Medical Coding: A Next-Generation Agentic System for Scalable and Explainable Medical Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29709)
*Joakim Edin,Andreas Motzfeldt,Simon Flachs,Lars Maaløe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Symphony是一个基于推理的医疗编码系统，通过直接访问编码指南来模拟专家编码员的工作方式，实现跨编码系统的通用性和可解释性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗编码是将临床文档转换为标准化代码的关键任务，但目前主要依赖人工操作，效率低且易出错。现有自动化方法存在三个主要问题：1) 只能预测固定代码集，无法适应新代码或不同编码系统；2) 需要针对不同系统重新训练；3) 缺乏预测解释，在安全关键场景中信任度有限。

Method: Symphony采用与专家编码员相似的方法：通过直接访问编码指南对临床叙述进行推理。系统设计允许跨任何编码系统操作，并提供跨度级别的证据，将每个预测代码链接到支持它的文本部分。

Result: 在两个公共基准和三个真实世界数据集上评估，涵盖美国和美国住院、门诊、急诊和专科设置。Symphony在所有设置中都取得了最先进的结果，证明其作为自动化临床编码的灵活、可部署基础系统。

Conclusion: Symphony通过模拟人类专家推理过程，解决了现有自动化医疗编码系统的关键限制，实现了跨系统通用性、可解释性和部署就绪性，为自动化临床编码提供了灵活的基础框架。

Abstract: Medical coding translates free-text clinical documentation into standardized codes drawn from classification systems that contain tens of thousands of entries and are updated annually. It is central to billing, clinical research, and quality reporting, yet remains largely manual, slow, and error-prone. Existing automated approaches learn to predict a fixed set of codes from labeled data, thereby preventing adaptation to new codes or different coding systems without retraining on different data. They also provide no explanation for their predictions, limiting trust in safety-critical settings. We introduce Symphony for Medical Coding, a system that approaches the task the way expert human coders do: by reasoning over the clinical narrative with direct access to the coding guidelines. This design allows Symphony to operate across any coding system and to provide span-level evidence linking each predicted code to the text that supports it. We evaluate on two public benchmarks and three real-world datasets spanning inpatient, outpatient, emergency, and subspecialty settings across the United States and the United Kingdom. Symphony achieves state-of-the-art results across all settings, establishing itself as a flexible, deployment-ready foundation for automated clinical coding.

</details>


### [159] [Reinforced Reasoning for End-to-End Retrosynthetic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29723)
*Chenyang Zuo,Siqi Fan,Yizhen Luo,Zaiqing Nie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReTriP：一种端到端生成框架，将逆合成重新表述为直接思维链推理任务，通过路径一致的分子表示和渐进式训练课程，在长程规划中实现最先进性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逆合成规划方法依赖单步预测与外部搜索启发式的混合框架，破坏了局部分子转化与全局规划目标之间的逻辑一致性。需要将复杂的战略预见直接嵌入模型的化学推理中。

Method: 引入ReTriP端到端生成框架，将逆合成重新表述为直接思维链推理任务。建立路径一致的分子表示，采用渐进式训练课程：从推理蒸馏过渡到具有可验证奖励的强化学习，使逐步生成与实际路线效用对齐。

Result: 在RetroBench上的实证评估表明，ReTriP实现了最先进的性能，与混合基线相比，在长程规划中表现出卓越的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ReTriP通过将逆合成重新表述为端到端思维链推理任务，成功将战略预见嵌入化学推理，解决了传统混合框架中局部与全局规划脱节的问题，在长程规划中展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Retrosynthetic planning is a fundamental task in organic chemistry, yet remains challenging due to its combinatorial complexity. To address this, conventional approaches typically rely on hybrid frameworks that combine single-step predictions with external search heuristics, inevitably fracturing the logical coherence between local molecular transformations and global planning objectives. To bridge this gap and embed sophisticated strategic foresight directly into the model's chemical reasoning, we introduce ReTriP, an end-to-end generative framework that reformulates retrosynthesis as a direct Chain-of-Thought reasoning task. We establish a path-coherent molecular representation and employ a progressive training curriculum that transitions from reasoning distillation to reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, effectively aligning stepwise generation with practical route utility. Empirical evaluation on RetroBench demonstrates that ReTriP achieves state-of-the-art performance, exhibiting superior robustness in long-horizon planning compared to hybrid baselines.

</details>


### [160] [Spontaneous Functional Differentiation in Large Language Models: A Brain-Like Intelligence Economy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29735)
*Junjie Zhang,Zhen Shen,Gang Xiong,Xisong Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大语言模型自发形成协同核心，信息整合超越个体部分，类似人脑；中层展现协同处理，早期和晚期层依赖冗余；这种组织是动态的，随任务难度增加呈现物理相变；消除协同成分导致性能灾难性损失，证实其为抽象推理的物理实体


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过人工智能系统的智能演化来识别通用计算原理，探索大语言模型中是否存在类似人脑的协同信息处理机制

Method: 使用集成信息分解方法分析多种架构的大语言模型，测量不同层的信息整合模式，并通过消融实验验证协同成分的功能重要性

Result: 发现大语言模型自发形成协同核心，中层表现出协同处理，早期和晚期层依赖冗余；这种组织是动态的，随任务难度增加呈现物理相变；消除协同成分导致性能灾难性损失

Conclusion: 协同核心是抽象推理的物理实体，这一发现连接了人工与生物智能，表明信息整合的通用计算原理在两种系统中都存在

Abstract: The evolution of intelligence in artificial systems provides a unique opportunity to identify universal computational principles. Here we show that large language models spontaneously develop synergistic cores where information integration exceeds individual parts remarkably similar to the human brain. Using Integrated Information Decomposition across multiple architectures we find that middle layers exhibit synergistic processing while early and late layers rely on redundancy. This organization is dynamic and emerges as a physical phase transition as task difficulty increases. Crucially ablating synergistic components causes catastrophic performance loss confirming their role as the physical entity of abstract reasoning and bridging artificial and biological intelligence.

</details>


### [161] [CausalPulse: An Industrial-Grade Neurosymbolic Multi-Agent Copilot for Causal Diagnostics in Smart Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29755)
*Chathurangi Shyalika,Utkarshani Jaimini,Cory Henson,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CausalPulse是一个面向智能制造的工业级多智能体协同系统，通过神经符号架构统一异常检测、因果发现和推理，在博世工厂实现实时部署，在多个数据集上达到98%以上的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代制造环境需要实时、可信且可解释的根因分析来维持生产力和质量。传统分析流程将异常检测、因果推断和根因分析作为孤立阶段处理，限制了可扩展性和可解释性。

Method: CausalPulse采用基于标准化智能体协议的神经符号架构，统一异常检测、因果发现和推理，构建多智能体协同系统，支持与现有监控工作流无缝集成。

Result: 在公开数据集(Future Factories)和专有数据集(Planar Sensor Element)上分别达到98.0%和98.73%的整体成功率；各准则成功率：规划和工具使用98.75%，自我反思97.3%，协作99.2%；端到端延迟50-60秒，近线性扩展性(R²=0.97)。

Conclusion: CausalPulse的模块化、人在回路设计为下一代制造提供了可靠、可解释且生产就绪的自动化解决方案，在模块化、可扩展性和部署成熟度方面优于现有工业协同系统。

Abstract: Modern manufacturing environments demand real-time, trustworthy, and interpretable root-cause insights to sustain productivity and quality. Traditional analytics pipelines often treat anomaly detection, causal inference, and root-cause analysis as isolated stages, limiting scalability and explainability. In this work, we present CausalPulse, an industry-grade multi-agent copilot that automates causal diagnostics in smart manufacturing. It unifies anomaly detection, causal discovery, and reasoning through a neurosymbolic architecture built on standardized agentic protocols. CausalPulse is being deployed in a Robert Bosch manufacturing plant, integrating seamlessly with existing monitoring workflows and supporting real-time operation at production scale. Evaluations on both public (Future Factories) and proprietary (Planar Sensor Element) datasets show high reliability, achieving overall success rates of 98.0% and 98.73%. Per-criterion success rates reached 98.75% for planning and tool use, 97.3% for self-reflection, and 99.2% for collaboration. Runtime experiments report end-to-end latency of 50-60s per diagnostic workflow with near-linear scalability (R^2=0.97), confirming real-time readiness. Comparison with existing industrial copilots highlights distinct advantages in modularity, extensibility, and deployment maturity. These results demonstrate how CausalPulse's modular, human-in-the-loop design enables reliable, interpretable, and production-ready automation for next-generation manufacturing.

</details>


### [162] [Tracking vs. Deciding: The Dual-Capability Bottleneck in Searchless Chess Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29761)
*Quanhao Li,Wei Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种通过序列训练构建类人象棋引擎的方法，揭示了状态追踪和决策质量之间的双重能力瓶颈，并通过模型缩放和Elo加权训练实现了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建类人象棋引擎的目标是模仿强人类棋手的风格、错误和一致性，而非单纯追求最大棋力。现有方法通常基于单个局面进行训练，无法捕捉完整对局历史中的决策模式。

Method: 1) 仅使用走子序列进行训练，迫使模型学习状态追踪（从历史走子重建棋盘）和决策质量（从重建状态选择好棋）两种能力；2) 提出双重能力瓶颈理论P ≤ min(T,Q)；3) 采用模型缩放（28M到120M参数）提升追踪能力；4) 引入Elo加权训练平衡高质量决策信号与多样性需求；5) 提出覆盖衰减公式t* = log(N/kcrit)/log b评估对局内退化风险。

Result: 最终120M参数模型（无搜索）在Lichess快棋达到2570等级分（253局对局）。人类走子预测Top-1准确率达55.2%，超过Maia-2快棋和Maia-2闪电战模型。2×2因子消融实验显示模型缩放提升追踪能力，加权训练提升决策质量，两者结合具有超加性效应。

Conclusion: 序列输入自然编码完整对局历史，使模型能够做出依赖历史的决策，这是单局面模型无法实现的。低等级对局提供多样性对状态追踪至关重要，而高等级对局提供决策质量信号，两者平衡是构建类人引擎的关键。

Abstract: A human-like chess engine should mimic the style, errors, and consistency of a strong human player rather than maximize playing strength. We show that training from move sequences alone forces a model to learn two capabilities: state tracking, which reconstructs the board from move history, and decision quality, which selects good moves from that reconstructed state. These impose contradictory data requirements: low-rated games provide the diversity needed for tracking, while high-rated games provide the quality signal for decision learning. Removing low-rated data degrades performance.
  We formalize this tension as a dual-capability bottleneck, P <= min(T,Q), where overall performance is limited by the weaker capability. Guided by this view, we scale the model from 28M to 120M parameters to improve tracking, then introduce Elo-weighted training to improve decisions while preserving diversity. A 2 x 2 factorial ablation shows that scaling improves tracking, weighting improves decisions, and their combination is superadditive. Linear weighting works best, while overly aggressive weighting harms tracking despite lower validation loss. We also introduce a coverage-decay formula, t* = log(N/kcrit)/log b, as a reliability horizon for intra-game degeneration risk.
  Our final 120M-parameter model, without search, reached Lichess bullet 2570 over 253 rated games. On human move prediction it achieves 55.2% Top-1 accuracy, exceeding Maia-2 rapid and Maia-2 blitz. Unlike position-based methods, sequence input naturally encodes full game history, enabling history-dependent decisions that single-position models cannot exhibit.

</details>


### [163] [Reasoning-Driven Synthetic Data Generation and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29791)
*Tim R. Davidson,Benoit Seguin,Enrico Bacis,Cesar Ilharco,Hamza Harkous*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Simula是一个无种子、基于智能体推理的框架，用于大规模生成和评估合成数据，通过可解释和可控的过程定义数据集特性，解决多模态AI训练数据稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态AI应用需要专门模型，但相关训练数据稀缺或难以获取。人工标注成本高、易出错且耗时，现有合成数据生成方法依赖人工提示、进化算法或大量目标分布种子数据，限制了可扩展性、可解释性和可控性。

Method: 提出Simula框架：采用无种子、基于智能体的方法大规模生成合成数据集，允许用户通过可解释和可控的过程定义期望的数据集特性，实现细粒度资源分配。

Result: 在多种数据集上验证了方法的有效性，严格测试了内在和下游特性。为合成数据机制设计提供指导，为大规模生成和评估合成数据提供见解，为数据稀缺或隐私敏感领域的AI开发部署开辟新机会。

Conclusion: Simula框架解决了多模态AI训练数据稀缺问题，通过可解释、可控的无种子智能体方法生成合成数据，为数据稀缺或隐私敏感领域的AI应用开发提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Although many AI applications of interest require specialized multi-modal models, relevant data to train such models is inherently scarce or inaccessible. Filling these gaps with human annotators is prohibitively expensive, error-prone, and time-consuming, leading model builders to increasingly consider synthetic data as a scalable alternative. However, existing synthetic data generation methods often rely on manual prompts, evolutionary algorithms, or extensive seed data from the target distribution - limiting their scalability, explainability, and control. In this paper, we introduce Simula: a novel reasoning-driven framework for data generation and evaluation. It employs a seedless, agentic approach to generate synthetic datasets at scale, allowing users to define desired dataset characteristics through an explainable and controllable process that enables fine-grained resource allocation. We show the efficacy of our approach on a variety of datasets, rigorously testing both intrinsic and downstream properties. Our work (1) offers guidelines for synthetic data mechanism design, (2) provides insights into generating and evaluating synthetic data at scale, and (3) unlocks new opportunities for developing and deploying AI in domains where data scarcity or privacy concerns are paramount.

</details>


### [164] [AgentFixer: From Failure Detection to Fix Recommendations in LLM Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29848)
*Hadar Mulian,Sergey Zeltyn,Ido Levy,Liane Galanti,Avi Yaeli,Segev Shlomov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个用于LLM智能体系统的综合验证框架，包含15个故障检测工具和2个根因分析模块，通过规则检查和LLM评估来诊断和改进可靠性问题，应用于IBM CUGA系统并显著提升中型模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体系统在生产环境中面临可靠性问题，缺乏系统化的验证框架来诊断和改进故障。需要一种能够系统检测输入处理、提示设计和输出生成等方面弱点的方法，以提升智能体系统的鲁棒性和可解释性。

Method: 开发了一个综合验证框架，包含15个故障检测工具和2个根因分析模块，结合轻量级规则检查和LLM-as-a-judge评估。在IBM CUGA系统上应用该框架，使用AppWorld和WebArena基准进行评估。通过诊断输出进行自我反思和优先级排序的探索性研究。

Result: 框架成功识别了规划器错位、模式违规、脆弱的提示依赖等重复性问题。基于这些洞察改进了提示和编码策略，在保持CUGA基准结果的同时，使Llama 4和Mistral Medium等中型模型实现了显著的准确率提升，大幅缩小了与前沿模型的差距。探索性研究产生了关于重复故障模式和改进重点的可操作洞察。

Conclusion: 该验证框架为生产智能体系统提供了可扩展、质量保证和自适应验证的路径，为构建更鲁棒、可解释和自我改进的智能体架构奠定了基础。验证本身可以演变为一个智能的、对话驱动的过程。

Abstract: We introduce a comprehensive validation framework for LLM-based agentic systems that provides systematic diagnosis and improvement of reliability failures. The framework includes fifteen failure-detection tools and two root-cause analysis modules that jointly uncover weaknesses across input handling, prompt design, and output generation. It integrates lightweight rule-based checks with LLM-as-a-judge assessments to support structured incident detection, classification, and repair. We applied the framework to IBM CUGA, evaluating its performance on the AppWorld and WebArena benchmarks. The analysis revealed recurrent planner misalignments, schema violations, brittle prompt dependencies, and more. Based on these insights, we refined both prompting and coding strategies, maintaining CUGA's benchmark results while enabling mid-sized models such as Llama 4 and Mistral Medium to achieve notable accuracy gains, substantially narrowing the gap with frontier models. Beyond quantitative validation, we conducted an exploratory study that fed the framework's diagnostic outputs and agent description into an LLM for self-reflection and prioritization. This interactive analysis produced actionable insights on recurring failure patterns and focus areas for improvement, demonstrating how validation itself can evolve into an agentic, dialogue-driven process. These results show a path toward scalable, quality assurance, and adaptive validation in production agentic systems, offering a foundation for more robust, interpretable, and self-improving agentic architectures.

</details>


### [165] [Spatiotemporal Robustness of Temporal Logic Tasks using Multi-Objective Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29868)
*Oliver Schön,Lars Lindemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出时空鲁棒性(STR)概念，通过多目标推理同时处理时空扰动，为交互系统提供更全面的鲁棒性评估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒性语义仅考虑空间扰动（信号几何距离），而交互系统（多机器人、智慧城市、空中交通管制）需要同时考虑时空扰动。需要更全面的鲁棒性评估框架来捕捉时空联合扰动。

Method: 1. 定义时空鲁棒性(STR)为多目标推理问题，通过时空扰动偏序形式化；2. 将STR解释为帕累托最优集，表征所有可接受的时空扰动；3. 提出可计算的鲁棒语义，在计算可行条件下合理近似STR；4. 基于这些语义开发监控算法。

Result: 首次通过多目标推理处理多维度鲁棒性，建立了时空鲁棒性理论框架，提供了可计算的鲁棒语义和监控算法，为交互系统鲁棒性评估提供了新方法。

Conclusion: 提出的时空鲁棒性框架能够更全面地评估系统在时空扰动下的鲁棒性，特别适用于交互系统。通过多目标推理方法，首次实现了跨多个维度的鲁棒性分析，为自主系统可靠性提供了更强大的理论工具。

Abstract: The reliability of autonomous systems depends on their robustness, i.e., their ability to meet their objectives under uncertainty. In this paper, we study spatiotemporal robustness of temporal logic specifications evaluated over discrete-time signals. Existing work has proposed robust semantics that capture not only Boolean satisfiability, but also the geometric distance from unsatisfiability, corresponding to admissible spatial perturbations of a given signal. In contrast, we propose spatiotemporal robustness (STR), which captures admissible spatial and temporal perturbations jointly. This notion is particularly informative for interacting systems, such as multi-agent robotics, smart cities, and air traffic control. We define STR as a multi-objective reasoning problem, formalized via a partial order over spatial and temporal perturbations. This perspective has two key advantages: (1) STR can be interpreted as a Pareto-optimal set that characterizes all admissible spatiotemporal perturbations, and (2) STR can be computed using tools from multi-objective optimization. To navigate computational challenges, we propose robust semantics for STR that are sound in the sense of suitably under-approximating STR while being computationally tractable. Finally, we present monitoring algorithms for STR using these robust semantics. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to deal with robustness across multiple dimensions via multi-objective reasoning.

</details>


### [166] [A Rational Account of Categorization Based on Information Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29895)
*Christophe J. MacLellan,Karthik Singaravadivelan,Xin Lian,Zekun Wang,Pat Langley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于信息论理性分析的新分类理论，在经典分类实验中优于现有模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个基于信息论理性分析的新分类理论，旨在更好地解释人类分类行为，并评估其相对于现有模型的优越性

Method: 采用信息论理性分析框架构建分类理论，在Hayes-Roth和Hayes-Roth(1977)、Medin和Schaffer(1978)、Smith和Minda(1998)的经典分类实验数据上进行评估

Result: 新理论解释人类分类行为的表现至少与独立线索和上下文模型(Medin & Schaffer, 1978)、分类理性模型(Anderson, 1991)以及分层狄利克雷过程模型(Griffiths et al., 2007)相当或更好

Conclusion: 基于信息论理性分析的新分类理论为人类分类行为提供了有效的解释框架，在多个经典实验数据集上表现出优于或至少不亚于现有主流模型的性能

Abstract: We present a new theory of categorization based on an information-theoretic rational analysis. To evaluate this theory, we investigate how well it can account for key findings from classic categorization experiments conducted by Hayes-Roth and Hayes-Roth (1977), Medin and Schaffer (1978), and Smith and Minda (1998). We find that it explains the human categorization behavior at least as well (or better) than the independent cue and context models (Medin & Schaffer, 1978), the rational model of categorization (Anderson, 1991), and a hierarchical Dirichlet process model (Griffiths et al., 2007).

</details>


### [167] [C-TRAIL: A Commonsense World Framework for Trajectory Planning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29908)
*Zhihong Cui,Haoran Tang,Tianyi Li,Yushuai Li,Peiyuan Guan,Amir Taherkordi,Tor Skeie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: C-TRAIL是一个用于自动驾驶轨迹规划的框架，通过结合LLM的常识推理和信任机制，在闭环的Recall-Plan-Update循环中实现可靠规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶轨迹规划中越来越多地使用大语言模型进行常识推理，但LLM输出具有固有的不可靠性，在安全关键应用中存在风险。

Method: C-TRAIL框架基于常识世界构建，通过Recall模块查询LLM语义关系并量化可靠性，Plan模块将信任加权的常识注入MCTS，Update模块从环境反馈中自适应优化信任分数和策略参数。

Result: 在Highway-env的四个模拟场景和两个真实世界数据集(highD, rounD)上的实验表明，C-TRAIL持续优于最先进的基线方法，平均减少ADE 40.2%、FDE 51.7%，提高SR 16.9个百分点。

Conclusion: C-TRAIL通过将LLM的常识推理与信任机制相结合，为自动驾驶轨迹规划提供了可靠且高效的解决方案，显著提升了规划性能。

Abstract: Trajectory planning for autonomous driving increasingly leverages large language models (LLMs) for commonsense reasoning, yet LLM outputs are inherently unreliable, posing risks in safety-critical applications. We propose C-TRAIL, a framework built on a Commonsense World that couples LLM-derived commonsense with a trust mechanism to guide trajectory planning. C-TRAIL operates through a closed-loop Recall, Plan, and Update cycle: the Recall module queries an LLM for semantic relations and quantifies their reliability via a dual-trust mechanism; the Plan module injects trust-weighted commonsense into Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) through a Dirichlet trust policy; and the Update module adaptively refines trust scores and policy parameters from environmental feedback. Experiments on four simulated scenarios in Highway-env and two real-world levelXData datasets (highD, rounD) show that C-TRAIL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, reducing ADE by 40.2%, FDE by 51.7%, and improving SR by 16.9 percentage points on average. The source code is available at https://github.com/ZhihongCui/CTRAIL.

</details>


### [168] [ScoringBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Tabular Foundation Models with Proper Scoring Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29928)
*Jonas Landsgesell,Pascal Knoll*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScoringBench是一个用于评估表格基础模型概率预测质量的开放基准，通过全面的评分规则（CRPS、CRLS、区间评分等）补充传统点估计指标，揭示模型在分布尾部的性能差异，对金融和临床研究等高风险决策至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前表格基础模型（如TabPFN和TabICL）虽然能产生完整的预测分布，但主流回归基准几乎只使用点估计指标（RMSE、R²）进行评估。这些聚合指标往往掩盖了模型在分布尾部的性能表现，而尾部性能对于金融和临床研究等高风险决策领域至关重要，因为这些领域通常存在不对称的风险特征。

Method: 引入ScoringBench开放基准，计算全面的适当评分规则套件，包括CRPS、CRLS、区间评分、能量评分、加权CRPS和Brier评分，同时保留标准点估计指标。评估了realTabPFNv2.5（使用不同评分规则目标进行微调）和TabICL相对于未微调的realTabPFNv2.5在一系列回归基准上的表现。

Result: 研究结果证实：1）模型排名取决于所选的评分规则；2）没有单一的预训练目标在所有情况下都是最优的。这表明对于对极端事件敏感的应用，评估指标的选择与数据本身一样，是领域特定的要求。

Conclusion: ScoringBench通过提供更丰富的概率预测质量评估框架，揭示了传统点估计指标的局限性，强调了在高风险决策场景中需要根据具体领域需求选择合适的评估指标。该基准通过Git拉取请求维护，确保透明度、可追溯性、敏捷性和可重复性。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models such as TabPFN and TabICL already produce full predictive distributions yet prevailing regression benchmarks evaluate them almost exclusively via point estimate metrics RMSE R2 These aggregate measures often obscure model performance in the tails of the distribution a critical deficit for high stakes decision making in domains like finance and clinical research where asymmetric risk profiles are the norm We introduce ScoringBench an open benchmark that computes a comprehensive suite of proper scoring rules like CRPS CRLS Interval Score Energy Score weighted CRPS and Brier Score alongside standard point metrics providing a richer picture of probabilistic forecast quality We evaluate realTabPFNv2.5 fine tuned with different scoring rule objectives and TabICL relative to untuned realTabPFNv2.5 across a suite of regression benchmarks Our results confirm that model rankings depend on the chosen scoring rule and that no single pretraining objective is universally optimal This demonstrates that for applications sensitive to extreme events the choice of evaluation metric is as much a domain specific requirement as the data itself ScoringBench is available at https://github.com/jonaslandsgesell/ScoringBench A live preview of the current leaderboard is available at https://scoringbench.bolt.host The leaderboard is maintained via git pull requests to ensure transparency traceability agility and reproducibility

</details>


### [169] [Physiological and Semantic Patterns in Medical Teams Using an Intelligent Tutoring System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29950)
*Xiaoshan Huang,Conrad Borchers,Jiayi Zhang,Susanne P. Lajoie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过融合生理同步性和对话语义分析，探索医疗团队协作中的关键调节时刻，发现生理同步峰值与语义多样性相关，可作为团队问题解决中"关键转折点"的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 团队协作需要社会共享学习调节（SSRL）来管理复杂的认知和情感状态。生理同步性可以指示这些状态，但单独难以解释。研究旨在通过融合生理信号和对话分析来理解问题解决中的关键时刻。

Method: 研究四个医疗双人团队使用智能辅导系统诊断虚拟患者案例。分析生理同步性（纵向生理信号对齐）和对话动态。将话语片段编码为SSRL类别，使用句子嵌入计算余弦相似度。进行定性和定量分析，将生理同步峰值与语义变化关联。

Result: 激活先验知识的语义相似度显著低于简单任务执行。高生理同步性与较低的语义相似度相关，表明这些时刻涉及探索性和多样化的语言使用。定性分析将同步峰值识别为"关键转折点"：成功团队在共享发现时同步，不成功团队在共享不确定性时达到峰值。

Conclusion: 研究表明生物信号与对话融合可以理解问题解决中的关键时刻，推进了以人为本的人工智能研究。生理同步性峰值与语义多样性相关，可作为团队协作中关键调节时刻的指标。

Abstract: Effective collaboration requires teams to manage complex cognitive and emotional states through Socially Shared Regulation of Learning (SSRL). Physiological synchrony (i.e., longitudinal alignment in physiological signals) can indicate these states, but is hard to interpret on its own. We investigate the physiological and conversational dynamics of four medical dyads diagnosing a virtual patient case using an intelligent tutoring system. Semantic shifts in dialogue were correlated with transient physiological synchrony peaks. We also coded utterance segments for SSRL and derived cosine similarity using sentence embeddings. The results showed that activating prior knowledge featured significantly lower semantic similarity than simpler task execution. High physiological synchrony was associated with lower semantic similarity, suggesting that such moments involve exploratory and varied language use. Qualitative analysis triangulated these synchrony peaks as ``pivotal moments'': successful teams synchronized during shared discovery, while unsuccessful teams peaked during shared uncertainty. This research advances human-centered AI by demonstrating how biological signals can be fused with dialogues to understand critical moments in problem solving.

</details>


### [170] [Structured Intent as a Protocol-Like Communication Layer: Cross-Model Robustness, Framework Comparison, and the Weak-Model Compensation Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29953)
*Peng Gang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 结构化意图表示（如5W3H、CO-STAR、RISEN）能显著提升AI模型跨语言、跨模型的用户目标对齐稳定性，减少语言间评分方差，并在用户研究中提高交互效率60%和满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究结构化意图表示在不同AI模型、语言和提示框架中保持用户目标的可靠性，验证其作为人机交互稳健通信层的实用价值。

Method: 三方面扩展研究：1) 跨模型鲁棒性测试（Claude、GPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Pro）；2) 与CO-STAR和RISEN的对照比较；3) 生态有效场景下的用户研究（N=50）。共分析3,240个模型输出，使用独立评估器DeepSeek-V3评分。

Result: 结构化提示显著降低跨语言评分方差（σ从0.470降至约0.020）；弱模型补偿效应明显（Gemini提升+1.006 vs Claude +0.217）；三种结构化方法目标对齐得分相近；用户研究中AI扩展的5W3H提示减少60%交互轮次，满意度从3.16提升至4.04。

Conclusion: 结构化意图表示作为人机交互的协议式通信层具有实际价值，维度分解本身是关键活性成分，能提升跨模型、跨语言的鲁棒性和用户交互效率。

Abstract: How reliably can structured intent representations preserve user goals across different AI models, languages, and prompting frameworks? Prior work showed that PPS (Prompt Protocol Specification), a 5W3H-based structured intent framework, improves goal alignment in Chinese and generalizes to English and Japanese. This paper extends that line of inquiry in three directions: cross-model robustness across Claude, GPT-4o, and Gemini 2.5 Pro; controlled comparison with CO-STAR and RISEN; and a user study (N=50) of AI-assisted intent expansion in ecologically valid settings. Across 3,240 model outputs (3 languages x 6 conditions x 3 models x 3 domains x 20 tasks), evaluated by an independent judge (DeepSeek-V3), we find that structured prompting substantially reduces cross-language score variance relative to unstructured baselines. The strongest structured conditions reduce cross-language sigma from 0.470 to about 0.020. We also observe a weak-model compensation pattern: the lowest-baseline model (Gemini) shows a much larger D-A gain (+1.006) than the strongest model (Claude, +0.217). Under the current evaluation resolution, 5W3H, CO-STAR, and RISEN achieve similarly high goal-alignment scores, suggesting that dimensional decomposition itself is an important active ingredient. In the user study, AI-expanded 5W3H prompts reduce interaction rounds by 60 percent and increase user satisfaction from 3.16 to 4.04. These findings support the practical value of structured intent representation as a robust, protocol-like communication layer for human-AI interaction.

</details>


### [171] [Extending MONA in Camera Dropbox: Reproduction, Learned Approval, and Design Implications for Reward-Hacking Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29993)
*Nathan Heath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究对MONA方法进行了复现和扩展，验证了原始论文结果，并探索了不同批准机制对安全保证的影响，发现学习型批准模型面临优化不足而非奖励黑客的新挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决MONA原始论文中关于批准机制如何影响安全保证的关键开放性问题，通过复现和扩展实验来验证批准谱系猜想。

Method: 复现MONA Camera Dropbox环境，将其重新打包为标准Python项目，使用脚本化PPO训练，引入模块化的学习批准套件（包括oracle、带噪声、错误指定、学习和校准批准机制），在不同批准方法、规划视野、数据集大小和校准策略上进行实验。

Result: 确认了原始论文结果：普通RL有91.5%的奖励黑客率，oracle MONA为0.0%。最佳校准学习型监督器运行实现了零奖励黑客，但预期行为率显著低于oracle MONA（11.9% vs. 99.9%），表现为优化不足而非奖励黑客重新出现。

Conclusion: 该研究将MONA的批准谱系猜想转化为可运行的实验对象，表明核心工程挑战从证明MONA概念转向构建既能保持足够远见又不重新开放奖励黑客渠道的学习型批准模型。

Abstract: Myopic Optimization with Non-myopic Approval (MONA) mitigates multi-step reward hacking by restricting the agent's planning horizon while supplying far-sighted approval as a training signal~\cite{farquhar2025mona}. The original paper identifies a critical open question: how the method of constructing approval -- particularly the degree to which approval depends on achieved outcomes -- affects whether MONA's safety guarantees hold. We present a reproduction-first extension of the public MONA Camera Dropbox environment that (i)~repackages the released codebase as a standard Python project with scripted PPO training, (ii)~confirms the published contrast between ordinary RL (91.5\% reward-hacking rate) and oracle MONA (0.0\% hacking rate) using the released reference arrays, and (iii)~introduces a modular learned-approval suite spanning oracle, noisy, misspecified, learned, and calibrated approval mechanisms. In reduced-budget pilot sweeps across approval methods, horizons, dataset sizes, and calibration strategies, the best calibrated learned-overseer run achieves zero observed reward hacking but substantially lower intended-behavior rates than oracle MONA (11.9\% vs.\ 99.9\%), consistent with under-optimization rather than re-emergent hacking. These results operationalize the MONA paper's approval-spectrum conjecture as a runnable experimental object and suggest that the central engineering challenge shifts from proving MONA's concept to building learned approval models that preserve sufficient foresight without reopening reward-hacking channels. Code, configurations, and reproduction commands are publicly available. https://github.com/codernate92/mona-camera-dropbox-repro

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [CREST: Constraint-Release Execution for Multi-Robot Warehouse Shelf Rearrangement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28803)
*Jiaqi Tan,Yudong Luo,Sophia Huang,Yifan Yang,Hang Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CREST框架通过在执行过程中主动释放轨迹约束，解决了DD-MAPD问题中MAPF-DECOMP方法因严格轨迹依赖导致的执行效率低下问题，显著提升了货架搬运的连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAPF-DECOMP框架在解决双甲板多智能体拾取交付问题时，虽然计算效率高，但强制执行严格的轨迹依赖关系，导致智能体空闲和货架切换频繁，执行质量较差。

Method: 提出CREST执行框架，在执行过程中主动释放轨迹约束，允许智能体更灵活地执行任务，从而提高货架搬运的连续性，减少空闲时间和不必要的货架切换。

Result: 在多种仓库布局上的实验表明，CREST相比MAPF-DECOMP在智能体移动距离、完工时间和货架切换次数等指标上分别减少了40.5%、33.3%和44.4%，在提升/放置开销下效果更显著。

Conclusion: 执行感知的约束释放对于可扩展的仓库重排至关重要，CREST框架通过更灵活的约束管理显著提升了多机器人货架重排的执行效率。

Abstract: Double-Deck Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery (DD-MAPD) models the multi-robot shelf rearrangement problem in automated warehouses. MAPF-DECOMP is a recent framework that first computes collision-free shelf trajectories with a MAPF solver and then assigns agents to execute them. While efficient, it enforces strict trajectory dependencies, often leading to poor execution quality due to idle agents and unnecessary shelf switching. We introduce CREST, a new execution framework that achieves more continuous shelf carrying by proactively releasing trajectory constraints during execution. Experiments on diverse warehouse layouts show that CREST consistently outperforms MAPF-DECOMP, reducing metrics related to agent travel, makespan, and shelf switching by up to 40.5\%, 33.3\%, and 44.4\%, respectively, with even greater benefits under lift/place overhead. These results underscore the importance of execution-aware constraint release for scalable warehouse rearrangement. Code and data are available at https://github.com/ChristinaTan0704/CREST.

</details>


### [173] [A Classification of Heterogeneity in Uncrewed Vehicle Swarms and the Effects of Its Inclusion on Overall Swarm Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28831)
*Abhishek Joshi,Abhishek Phadke,Tianxing Chu,F. Antonio Medrano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个系统框架，用于对异构无人载具集群进行分类，基于智能体性质、硬件结构和操作空间三个主要因素，并分析了异构集群在提升任务弹性和操作能力方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构无人载具集群通过组合不同类型的智能体来增强任务弹性和操作能力，但缺乏系统性的分类框架来指导设计和实施。本研究旨在填补这一空白，为异构集群提供统一的分类体系和设计指导。

Method: 研究提出了基于三个主要因素的分类框架：1) 智能体性质（行为和功能），2) 硬件结构（物理配置和感知能力），3) 操作空间（操作领域）。通过文献综述分析异构集群的性能优势，并讨论操作挑战如通信架构约束、能量感知协调策略和控制系统集成。

Result: 分析表明异构集群具有更高的弹性，能够利用多样化能力、动态调整角色并整合多维传感器数据。战略异构性显著提升集群性能。研究识别了实施中的关键因素：学习策略的仿真到现实转移、标准化评估指标和可互操作的控制架构。

Conclusion: 异构集群技术正接近高价值应用的成熟阶段。本研究提供了统一的分类法和基于证据的观察，指导设计任务就绪的异构集群，在复杂性和增强能力之间取得平衡。学习型协调、GPS拒止环境下的多机器人SLAM和特定领域商业部署共同推动该技术的发展。

Abstract: Combining different types of agents in uncrewed vehicle (UV) swarms has emerged as an approach to enhance mission resilience and operational capabilities across a wide range of applications. This study offers a systematic framework for grouping different types of swarms based on three main factors: agent nature (behavior and function), hardware structure (physical configuration and sensing capabilities), and operational space (domain of operation). A literature review indicates that strategic heterogeneity significantly improves swarm performance. Operational challenges, including communication architecture constraints, energy-aware coordination strategies, and control system integration, are also discussed. The analysis shows that heterogeneous swarms are more resilient because they can leverage diverse capabilities, adapt roles on the fly, and integrate data from multidimensional sensor feeds. Some important factors to consider when implementing are sim-to-real-world transfer for learned policies, standardized evaluation metrics, and control architectures that can work together. Learning-based coordination, GPS (Global Positioning System)-denied multi-robot SLAM (Simultaneous Localization and Mapping), and domain-specific commercial deployments collectively demonstrate that heterogeneous swarm technology is moving closer to readiness for high-value applications. This study offers a single taxonomy and evidence-based observations on methods for designing mission-ready heterogeneous swarms that balance complexity and increased capability.

</details>


### [174] [A Semantic Observer Layer for Autonomous Vehicles: Pre-Deployment Feasibility Study of VLMs for Low-Latency Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28888)
*Kunal Runwal,Swaraj Gajare,Daniel Adejumo,Omkar Ankalkope,Siddhant Baroth,Aliasghar Arab*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出语义观察层：量化视觉语言模型以1-2Hz频率运行，监控自动驾驶中的语义异常并触发安全切换


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义异常（像素级检测器无法推理的上下文相关危险）对自动驾驶安全构成关键风险，需要专门的监控机制

Method: 使用Nvidia Cosmos-Reason1-7B模型，采用NVFP4量化和FlashAttention2优化，构建语义观察层作为主控制循环的并行监控系统

Result: 实现约500ms推理时间，相比未优化的FP16基线加速约50倍；识别NF4量化导致10.6%召回率下降；建立性能指标与安全目标映射

Conclusion: 语义观察器架构在具身AI自动驾驶平台上具有部署可行性，为安全关键系统提供了预部署验证框架

Abstract: Semantic anomalies-context-dependent hazards that pixel-level detectors cannot reason about-pose a critical safety risk in autonomous driving. We propose a \emph{semantic observer layer}: a quantized vision-language model (VLM) running at 1--2\,Hz alongside the primary AV control loop, monitoring for semantic edge cases, and triggering fail-safe handoffs when detected. Using Nvidia Cosmos-Reason1-7B with NVFP4 quantization and FlashAttention2, we achieve ~500 ms inference a ~50x speedup over the unoptimized FP16 baseline (no quantization, standard PyTorch attention) on the same hardware--satisfying the observer timing budget. We benchmark accuracy, latency, and quantization behavior in static and video conditions, identify NF4 recall collapse (10.6%) as a hard deployment constraint, and a hazard analysis mapping performance metrics to safety goals. The results establish a pre-deployment feasibility case for the semantic observer architecture on embodied-AI AV platforms.

</details>


### [175] [Bootstrap Perception Under Hardware Depth Failure for Indoor Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28890)
*Nishant Pushparaju,Vivek Mattam,Aliasghar Arab*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对硬件深度传感器失效的自举感知系统，通过利用传感器自身有效像素校准学习式单目深度估计，在走廊等场景中填补深度缺失区域，实现鲁棒的室内机器人导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决室内机器人导航中硬件深度传感器（如ToF相机）在反射表面失效（深度像素丢失达78%）的问题，同时克服2D LiDAR无法感知扫描平面以上障碍物的局限性。

Method: 采用故障感知的层次化感知架构：以LiDAR作为几何锚点，保留硬件深度的有效区域，仅在需要时引入学习式深度估计。系统利用传感器自身有效像素校准学习式单目深度到度量尺度，实现自举式深度补全。

Result: 选择性融合使代价地图障碍物覆盖率比仅使用LiDAR提高55-110%。紧凑的蒸馏学生模型在Jetson Orin Nano上达到218 FPS，在闭环仿真中实现9/10导航成功率且零碰撞，性能匹配地面真实深度基线但成本显著降低。

Conclusion: 该系统通过自举方式利用传感器自身有效信息填补深度缺失，构建了故障感知的层次化感知架构，在硬件深度传感器失效时仍能保持鲁棒的导航性能，为资源受限的机器人平台提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: We present a bootstrap perception system for indoor robot navigation under hardware depth failure. In our corridor data, the time-of-flight camera loses up to 78% of its depth pixels on reflective surfaces, yet a 2D LiDAR alone cannot sense obstacles above its scan plane. Our system exploits a self-referential property of this failure: the sensor's surviving valid pixels calibrate learned monocular depth to metric scale, so the system fills its own gaps without external data. The architecture forms a failure-aware sensing hierarchy, conservative when sensors work and filling in when they fail: LiDAR remains the geometric anchor, hardware depth is kept where valid, and learned depth enters only where needed. In corridor and dynamic pedestrian evaluations, selective fusion increases costmap obstacle coverage by 55-110% over LiDAR alone. A compact distilled student runs at 218\,FPS on a Jetson Orin Nano and achieves 9/10 navigation success with zero collisions in closed-loop simulation, matching the ground-truth depth baseline at a fraction of the foundation model's cost.

</details>


### [176] [Robust Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Small UAS Separation Assurance under GPS Degradation and Spoofing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28900)
*Alex Zongo,Filippos Fotiadis,Ufuk Topcu,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用多智能体强化学习解决GPS退化/欺骗下的小型无人机系统鲁棒分离保障问题，通过零和博弈建模观测状态扰动，推导闭式对抗扰动表达式，实现线性时间评估，在35%腐败率下实现近零碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GPS退化或欺骗攻击下，小型无人机系统（sUAS）的位置广播信息可能被破坏，导致整个空中交通状态观测不可靠，威胁飞行安全。需要开发鲁棒的分离保障方法应对这种观测状态腐败问题。

Method: 将观测状态腐败建模为零和博弈：以概率R，对抗方扰动观测状态以最大化降低智能体安全性能。推导对抗扰动的闭式表达式，避免对抗训练，实现状态维度线性时间评估。将该表达式集成到MARL策略梯度算法中，获得鲁棒对抗策略。

Result: 闭式表达式能以二阶精度逼近真实最坏情况对抗扰动。在Kullback-Leibler正则化下，清洁与腐败观测间的安全性能差距最多以腐败概率线性退化。在高密度sUAS仿真中，在腐败率高达35%的情况下实现近零碰撞率，优于无对抗扰动训练的基线策略。

Conclusion: 提出的基于零和博弈和闭式对抗扰动表达式的MARL方法能有效应对GPS退化/欺骗下的观测状态腐败问题，为小型无人机系统提供鲁棒的分离保障，在较高腐败率下仍能保持飞行安全。

Abstract: We address robust separation assurance for small Unmanned Aircraft Systems (sUAS) under GPS degradation and spoofing via Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL). In cooperative surveillance, each aircraft (or agent) broadcasts its GPS-derived position; when such position broadcasts are corrupted, the entire observed air traffic state becomes unreliable. We cast this state observation corruption as a zero-sum game between the agents and an adversary: with probability R, the adversary perturbs the observed state to maximally degrade each agent's safety performance. We derive a closed-form expression for this adversarial perturbation, bypassing adversarial training entirely and enabling linear-time evaluation in the state dimension. We show that this expression approximates the true worst-case adversarial perturbation with second-order accuracy. We further bound the safety performance gap between clean and corrupted observations, showing that it degrades at most linearly with the corruption probability under Kullback-Leibler regularization. Finally, we integrate the closed-form adversarial policy into a MARL policy gradient algorithm to obtain a robust counter-policy for the agents. In a high-density sUAS simulation, we observe near-zero collision rates under corruption levels up to 35%, outperforming a baseline policy trained without adversarial perturbations.

</details>


### [177] [See Something, Say Something: Context-Criticality-Aware Mobile Robot Communication for Hazard Mitigations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28901)
*Bhavya Oza,Devam Shah,Ghanashyama Prabhu,Devika Kodi,Aliasghar Arab*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一个基于VLM/LLM感知的自适应紧急消息生成框架，通过系统评估危险程度、时间敏感性和缓解可行性来减少响应时间，在巡逻机器人上验证显示比固定优先级基线更快且用户信任度达82%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键场景中，自主移动机器人检测到危险时需要快速有效沟通，延迟或不恰当的响应会增加行动时间和风险，需要系统性的上下文敏感评估来减少响应时间并提高缓解效果

Method: 提出基于VLM/LLM感知的自适应消息生成框架，系统评估危险程度、时间敏感性和缓解可行性，根据上下文动态调整响应策略（如厨房中的刀平静确认，走廊中的刀触发紧急协调警报）

Result: 在60多次巡逻机器人运行中验证，不仅实现更快响应，用户信任度达到82%，相比固定优先级基线，结构化危险评估同时提高了响应速度和缓解效果

Conclusion: 结构化危险评估框架通过VLM/LLM驱动的自适应消息生成，能够有效减少响应时间并提高缓解效果，在安全关键场景中为自主移动机器人提供了更智能的沟通机制

Abstract: The proverb ``see something, say something'' captures a core responsibility of autonomous mobile robots in safety-critical situations: when they detect a hazard, they must communicate--and do so quickly. In emergency scenarios, delayed or miscalibrated responses directly increase the time to action and the risk of damage. We argue that a systematic context-sensitive assessment of the criticality level, time sensitivity, and feasibility of mitigation is necessary for AMRs to reduce time to action and respond effectively. This paper presents a framework in which VLM/LLM-based perception drives adaptive message generation, for example, a knife in a kitchen produces a calm acknowledgment; the same object in a corridor triggers an urgent coordinated alert. Validation in 60+ runs using a patrolling mobile robot not only empowers faster response, but also brings user trusts to 82\% compared to fixed-priority baselines, validating that structured criticality assessment improves both response speed and mitigation effectiveness.

</details>


### [178] [Why That Robot? A Qualitative Analysis of Justification Strategies for Robot Color Selection Across Occupational Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28919)
*Jiangen He,Wanqi Zhang,Jessica K. Barfield*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究用户如何合理化选择不同肤色和拟人化特征的机器人，发现功能主义是主要理由，但用户会调整实用理由以符合种族和职业刻板印象，且偏见常隐藏在意识理性化之下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人越来越多地进入劳动力市场，需要了解隐性社会偏见如何影响用户对机器人的偏好，特别是肤色和拟人化特征在不同职业背景下的选择合理化过程。

Method: 通过定性分析4,146个开放式理由（来自1,038名参与者），开发并验证了一个多维编码方案（人类-AI共识κ=0.73），研究了四个专业背景下机器人颜色选择的推理框架。

Result: 功能主义是主要合理化策略（52%），但参与者系统性地调整实用理由以符合种族和职业刻板印象；暴露于种族刻板印象线索显著改变了颜色选择，但口头理由仍被标准情感或任务相关推理掩盖；人口背景显著影响合理化策略；机器人形状强烈调节颜色解释，高度拟人化时用户从功能推理转向机器中心化的去种族化。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了隐性偏见如何影响机器人设计选择，提供了可操作的设计建议，有助于减少未来劳动力机器人中社会偏见的延续。

Abstract: As robots increasingly enter the workforce, human-robot interaction (HRI) must address how implicit social biases influence user preferences. This paper investigates how users rationalize their selections of robots varying in skin tone and anthropomorphic features across different occupations. By qualitatively analyzing 4,146 open-ended justifications from 1,038 participants, we map the reasoning frameworks driving robot color selection across four professional contexts. We developed and validated a comprehensive, multidimensional coding scheme via human--AI consensus ($κ= 0.73$). Our results demonstrate that while utilitarian \textit{Functionalism} is the dominant justification strategy (52\%), participants systematically adapted these practical rationales to align with established racial and occupational stereotypes. Furthermore, we reveal that bias frequently operates beneath conscious rationalization: exposure to racial stereotype primes significantly shifted participants' color choices, yet their spoken justifications remained masked by standard affective or task-related reasoning. We also found that demographic backgrounds significantly shape justification strategies, and that robot shape strongly modulates color interpretation. Specifically, as robots become highly anthropomorphic, users increasingly retreat from functional reasoning toward \textit{Machine-Centric} de-racialization. Through these empirical results, we provide actionable design implications to help reduce the perpetuation of societal biases in future workforce robots.

</details>


### [179] [World2Rules: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Learning World-Governing Safety Rules for Aviation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28952)
*Haichuan Wang,Jay Patrikar,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: World2Rules是一个从多模态航空数据中学习安全规则的神经符号框架，通过分层反射推理和一致性约束，生成可解释的一阶逻辑规则，在F1分数上优于纯神经和单次神经符号基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界安全关键系统的安全规则通常复杂且依赖上下文，手动制定不完整且容易出错。从多模态数据学习规则面临噪声、不一致性和稀疏故障案例的挑战。纯神经方法缺乏形式化保证，而符号方法对不完美观测数据脆弱。

Method: 提出World2Rules神经符号框架：1) 使用神经模型从文本和视觉数据中提取候选符号事实；2) 使用归纳逻辑编程作为验证层；3) 采用分层反射推理，在示例、子集和规则三个层次上强制一致性，过滤不可靠证据，聚合相互一致的组件，修剪不支持假设。

Result: 在真实世界航空安全数据上评估，World2Rules学习的规则比纯神经方法F1分数高23.6%，比单次神经符号基线高43.2%。生成的规则紧凑、可解释，适合安全关键推理和形式化分析。

Conclusion: World2Rules通过神经符号集成和分层一致性约束，有效解决了从噪声多模态数据中学习安全规则的挑战，提供了既有神经模型的数据提取能力又有符号方法形式化保证的解决方案。

Abstract: Many real-world safety-critical systems are governed by explicit rules that define unsafe world configurations and constrain agent interactions. In practice, these rules are complex and context-dependent, making manual specification incomplete and error-prone. Learning such rules from real-world multimodal data is further challenged by noise, inconsistency, and sparse failure cases. Neural models can extract structure from text and visual data but lack formal guarantees, while symbolic methods provide verifiability yet are brittle when applied directly to imperfect observations. We present World2Rules, a neuro-symbolic framework for learning world-governing safety rules from real-world multimodal aviation data. World2Rules learns from both nominal operational data and aviation crash and incident reports, treating neural models as proposal mechanisms for candidate symbolic facts and inductive logic programming as a verification layer. The framework employs hierarchical reflective reasoning, enforcing consistency across examples, subsets, and rules to filter unreliable evidence, aggregate only mutually consistent components, and prune unsupported hypotheses. This design limits error propagation from noisy neural extractions and yields compact, interpretable first-order logic rules that characterize unsafe world configurations. We evaluate World2Rules on real-world aviation safety data and show that it learns rules that achieve 23.6% higher F1 score than purely neural and 43.2% higher F1 score than single-pass neuro-symbolic baseline, while remaining suitable for safety-critical reasoning and formal analysis.

</details>


### [180] [AutoWorld: Scaling Multi-Agent Traffic Simulation with Self-Supervised World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28963)
*Mozhgan Pourkeshavatz,Tianran Liu,Nicholas Rhinehart*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AutoWorld：基于无标注LiDAR数据的交通仿真框架，通过世界模型学习占用表示，采用级联DPP采样提升多样性，在WOSAC基准上实现最佳真实感


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前数据驱动的交通仿真器严重依赖带标注轨迹或语义标注的监督学习，难以扩展性能；而大量无标注传感器数据未被充分利用。需要开发能够利用无标注数据提升交通仿真性能的方法。

Method: 1) 从无标注LiDAR数据的占用表示中学习世界模型；2) 基于世界模型样本构建粗到细的预测场景上下文作为多智能体运动生成模型的输入；3) 使用级联Determinantal Point Process框架指导世界模型和运动模型的采样过程以提升样本多样性；4) 设计运动感知的潜在监督目标增强场景动态表示能力。

Result: 在WOSAC基准测试中，AutoWorld在主要真实感元指标(RMM)上排名第一。实验表明，包含无标注LiDAR数据能持续提升仿真性能，各组件有效性通过消融实验得到验证。

Conclusion: AutoWorld为无需额外标注即可扩展交通仿真真实感提供了新途径，证明了利用无标注传感器数据提升仿真性能的可行性。

Abstract: Multi-agent traffic simulation is central to developing and testing autonomous driving systems. Recent data-driven simulators have achieved promising results, but rely heavily on supervised learning from labeled trajectories or semantic annotations, making it costly to scale their performance. Meanwhile, large amounts of unlabeled sensor data can be collected at scale but remain largely unused by existing traffic simulation frameworks. This raises a key question: How can a method harness unlabeled data to improve traffic simulation performance? In this work, we propose AutoWorld, a traffic simulation framework that employs a world model learned from unlabeled occupancy representations of LiDAR data. Given world model samples, AutoWorld constructs a coarse-to-fine predictive scene context as input to a multi-agent motion generation model. To promote sample diversity, AutoWorld uses a cascaded Determinantal Point Process framework to guide the sampling processes of both the world model and the motion model. Furthermore, we designed a motion-aware latent supervision objective that enhances AutoWorld's representation of scene dynamics. Experiments on the WOSAC benchmark show that AutoWorld ranks first on the leaderboard according to the primary Realism Meta Metric (RMM). We further show that simulation performance consistently improves with the inclusion of unlabeled LiDAR data, and study the efficacy of each component with ablations. Our method paves the way for scaling traffic simulation realism without additional labeling. Our project page contains additional visualizations and released code.

</details>


### [181] [Large Neighborhood Search for Multi-Agent Task Assignment and Path Finding with Precedence Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28968)
*Viraj Parimi,Brian C. Williams*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于任务分配与路径规划优先级约束问题（TAPF-PC）的大邻域搜索方法，通过迭代重分配改进固定任务分配的初始解


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多多机器人应用不仅需要高效的路径规划，还需要优化任务分配，因为解决方案质量不仅取决于机器人如何移动，还取决于哪个机器人执行哪个任务。现有的MAPF-PC框架假设任务序列固定，无法优化任务分配。

Method: 开发了一种大邻域搜索方法：从可行的MAPF-PC初始解开始，通过基于重分配的邻域修复迭代改进，在每个选定的邻域内恢复可行性。

Result: 在多个基准测试系列和扩展场景中的实验表明，最佳配置改进了89.1%的实例相对于固定分配初始解，证明大邻域搜索能有效捕获优先级约束下灵活重分配的收益。

Conclusion: 大邻域搜索方法能够有效解决TAPF-PC问题，通过联合优化任务分配、优先级满足和路径成本，显著提升解决方案质量。

Abstract: Many multi-robot applications require tasks to be completed efficiently and in the correct order, so that downstream operations can proceed at the right time. Multi-agent path finding with precedence constraints (MAPF-PC) is a well-studied framework for computing collision-free plans that satisfy ordering relations when task sequences are fixed in advance. In many applications, however, solution quality depends not only on how agents move, but also on which agent performs which task. This motivates the lifted problem of task assignment and path finding with precedence constraints (TAPF-PC), which extends MAPF-PC by jointly optimizing assignment, precedence satisfaction, and routing cost. To address the resulting coupled TAPF-PC search space, we develop a large neighborhood search approach that starts from a feasible MAPF-PC seed and iteratively improves it through reassignment-based neighborhood repair, restoring feasibility within each selected neighborhood. Experiments across multiple benchmark families and scaling regimes show that the best-performing configuration improves 89.1% of instances over fixed-assignment seed solutions, demonstrating that large neighborhood search effectively captures the gains from flexible reassignment under precedence constraints.

</details>


### [182] [Gleanmer: A 6 mW SoC for Real-Time 3D Gaussian Occupancy Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29005)
*Zih-Sing Fu,Peter Zhi Xuan Li,Sertac Karaman,Vivienne Sze*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Gleanmer是一款用于3D占用映射的SoC芯片，通过算法-硬件协同优化，在边缘设备上实现实时低功耗的3D高斯映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘应用（如AR/VR和自主导航）需要高保真度的3D占用映射，但受到功率限制的制约。现有方案在功耗和性能方面存在瓶颈。

Method: 提出Gleanmer SoC，包含针对GMMap（基于高斯分布的3D占用映射）的专用加速器。采用算法-硬件协同优化策略：1）直接计算和高效重用紧凑高斯分布；2）对高斯分布进行近似计算以减少面积。

Result: 在16nm CMOS工艺下：1）地图构建能耗降低63%，查询能耗降低81%；2）加速器面积减少38%；3）实时处理640x480图像，帧率超过88fps；4）地图查询每秒处理超过540K坐标；5）总功耗低于6mW。

Conclusion: Gleanmer是首个实现实时3D占用映射的SoC芯片，在低于6mW功耗下满足边缘应用需求，通过算法-硬件协同优化显著提升了能效和性能。

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D occupancy mapping is essential for many edge-based applications (such as AR/VR and autonomous navigation) but is limited by power constraints. We present Gleanmer, a system on chip (SoC) with an accelerator for GMMap, a 3D occupancy map using Gaussians. Through algorithm-hardware co-optimizations for direct computation and efficient reuse of these compact Gaussians, Gleanmer reduces construction and query energy by up to 63% and 81%, respectively. Approximate computation on Gaussians reduces accelerator area by 38%. Using 16nm CMOS, Gleanmer processes 640x480 images in real time beyond 88 fps during map construction and processes over 540K coordinates per second during map query. To our knowledge, Gleanmer is the first fabricated SoC to achieve real-time 3D occupancy mapping under 6 mW for edge-based applications.

</details>


### [183] [Efficient Camera Pose Augmentation for View Generalization in Robotic Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29192)
*Sen Wang,Huaiyi Dong,Jingyi Tian,Jiayi Li,Zhuo Yang,Tongtong Cao,Anlin Chen,Shuang Wu,Le Wang,Sanping Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GenSplat：基于3D高斯泼溅的视角泛化策略学习框架，通过新颖视角渲染增强策略的3D结构理解能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D视觉运动策略在未见视角上泛化能力不足，因为它们依赖静态观测，无法在不同视角间保持一致的行动映射

Method: 1) 使用置换等变架构从稀疏、未标定输入中单次前向传播重建高保真3D场景；2) 设计3D先验蒸馏策略正则化3DGS优化，防止纯光度监督导致的几何坍塌；3) 从稳定3D表示渲染多样化合成视角，系统性地增强训练时的观测流形

Result: 该方法迫使策略基于底层3D结构做出决策，在严重空间扰动下实现鲁棒执行，而基线方法性能严重下降

Conclusion: GenSplat通过3D场景重建和视角渲染，有效解决了2D策略的视角泛化问题，为视觉运动策略提供了更稳健的3D基础

Abstract: Prevailing 2D-centric visuomotor policies exhibit a pronounced deficiency in novel view generalization, as their reliance on static observations hinders consistent action mapping across unseen views. In response, we introduce GenSplat, a feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting framework that facilitates view-generalized policy learning through novel view rendering. GenSplat employs a permutation-equivariant architecture to reconstruct high-fidelity 3D scenes from sparse, uncalibrated inputs in a single forward pass. To ensure structural integrity, we design a 3D-prior distillation strategy that regularizes the 3DGS optimization, preventing the geometric collapse typical of purely photometric supervision. By rendering diverse synthetic views from these stable 3D representations, we systematically augment the observational manifold during training. This augmentation forces the policy to ground its decisions in underlying 3D structures, thereby ensuring robust execution under severe spatial perturbations where baselines severely degrade.

</details>


### [184] [Kilohertz-Safe: A Scalable Framework for Constrained Dexterous Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29213)
*Yinxiao Tian,Ziyi Yang,Zinan Zhao,Zhen Kan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种可扩展的运动重定向框架，将非线性重定向问题转化为关节微分空间中的凸二次规划问题，实现高频实时性能并保证形式化安全约束


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手遥操作需要同时实现高频实时性能和异构运动学与安全约束的运动重定向方法。现有非线性优化方法计算成本过高，难以应用于千赫兹级控制，而基于学习的方法通常缺乏形式化安全保证

Method: 将非线性重定向问题重新表述为关节微分空间中的凸二次规划问题，通过系统线性化纳入异构约束（包括运动学限制和碰撞避免），并集成控制屏障函数提供形式化安全保证

Result: 在Wuji Hand平台上通过仿真和硬件实验验证，优于Dex-Retargeting和GeoRT等最先进方法。框架实现高频操作，平均延迟9.05毫秒，超过95%的重定向帧满足安全标准，有效减轻复杂操作任务中的自碰撞和穿透

Conclusion: 提出的可扩展运动重定向框架在计算效率和形式化安全保证方面取得平衡，为灵巧手遥操作提供了一种高效可靠的运动重定向解决方案

Abstract: Dexterous hand teleoperation requires motion re-targeting methods that simultaneously achieve high-frequency real-time performance and enforcement of heterogeneous kinematic and safety constraints. Existing nonlinear optimization-based approaches often incur prohibitive computational cost, limiting their applicability to kilohertz-level control, while learning-based methods typically lack formal safety guarantees. This paper proposes a scalable motion retargeting framework that reformulates the nonlinear retargeting problem into a convex quadratic program in joint differential space. Heterogeneous constraints, including kinematic limits and collision avoidance, are incorporated through systematic linearization, resulting in improved computational efficiency and numerical stability. Control barrier functions are further integrated to provide formal safety guarantees during the retargeting process. The proposed framework is validated through simulations and hardware experiments on the Wuji Hand platform, outperforming state-of-the-art methods such as Dex-Retargeting and GeoRT. The framework achieves high-frequency operation with an average latency of 9.05 ms, while over 95% of retargeted frames satisfy the safety criteria, effectively mitigating self-collision and penetration during complex manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [185] [Long-Reach Robotic Manipulation for Assembly and Outfitting of Lunar Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29226)
*Stanley Wang,Venny Kojouharov,Long Yin Chung,Daniel Morton,Mark Cutkosky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于月球基础设施建设的紧凑型长臂机械臂，采用可展开复合材料臂，能在1.8米范围内实现小于15毫米的末端精度，适用于电缆布线等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球表面基础设施建设需要半自主或全自主机器人操作，特别是电缆布线等任务需要在大结构上进行运输、布线和精细操作，现有机械臂难以满足长距离、大工作空间的需求。

Method: 设计了一种集成可展开复合材料臂的紧凑型长臂机械臂，分析了可展开结构的挠度、振动和展开特性，并提出了相应的操作控制策略来抑制这些影响。

Result: 实验表明，在臂长达到1.8米时，平均末端精度误差小于15毫米，并通过电缆布线任务验证了该方案在月球设施建设应用中的潜力。

Conclusion: 所提出的可展开复合材料臂机械臂系统能够有效解决月球基础设施建设中的长距离操作需求，为月球电缆布线等任务提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Future infrastructure construction on the lunar surface will require semi- or fully-autonomous operation from robots deployed at the build site. In particular, tasks such as electrical outfitting necessitate transport, routing, and fine manipulation of cables across large structures. To address this need, we present a compact and long-reach manipulator incorporating a deployable composite boom, capable of performing manipulation tasks across large structures and workspaces. We characterize the deflection, vibration, and blossoming characteristics inherent to the deployable structure, and present a manipulation control strategy to mitigate these effects. Experiments indicate an average endpoint accuracy error of less than 15 mm for boom lengths up to 1.8 m. We demonstrate the approach with a cable routing task to illustrate the potential for lunar outfitting applications that benefit from long reach.

</details>


### [186] [Long-Reach Robotic Cleaning for Lunar Solar Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29240)
*Stanley Wang,Velin Kojouharov,Long Yin Chung,Daniel Morton,Mark Cutkosky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于月球太阳能板清洁的小型移动机器人，配备长臂可展开机械臂和可更换清洁工具，通过顺应性手腕和力控制实现稳定接触清洁


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业月球活动加速了对可靠表面基础设施的需求，维护任务如检查、清洁、除尘和小修对保持性能和延长系统寿命至关重要。月球太阳能板提供大部分表面电力，但随时间积累的月尘会迅速降低输出功率，缩短任务寿命。

Method: 设计小型移动机器人，配备轻量级可展开长臂机械臂和可更换清洁工具。在先前视觉引导长臂操作基础上，增加具有远端力感知的顺应性手腕和基于速度的导纳控制器，以调节表面清洁期间的稳定接触。

Result: 在平面上的初步实验表明，系统在0.3米到1.0米臂长范围内执行简单清洁运动时，能保持约2N的法向力，初始接触后的RMS力误差约为0.2N。

Conclusion: 早期结果表明，可展开长臂机械臂是用于月球基础设施（如太阳能板、散热器和光学表面）机器人维护的有前景的架构。

Abstract: Commercial lunar activity is accelerating the need for reliable surface infrastructure and routine operations to keep it functioning. Maintenance tasks such as inspection, cleaning, dust mitigation, and minor repair are essential to preserve performance and extend system life. A specific application is the cleaning of lunar solar arrays. Solar arrays are expected to provide substantial fraction of lunar surface power and operate for months to years, supplying continuous energy to landers, habitats, and surface assets, making sustained output mission-critical. However, over time lunar dust accumulates on these large solar arrays, which can rapidly degrade panel output and reduce mission lifetime. We propose a small mobile robot equipped with a long-reach, lightweight deployable boom and interchangeable cleaning tool to perform gentle cleaning over meter-scale workspaces with minimal human involvement. Building on prior vision-guided long-reach manipulation, we add a compliant wrist with distal force sensing and a velocity-based admittance controller to regulate stable contact during surface cleaning. In preliminary benchtop experiments on a planar surface, the system maintained approximately 2 N normal force while executing a simple cleaning motion over boom lengths from 0.3 m to 1.0 m, with RMS force error of approximately 0.2 N after initial contact. These early results suggest that deployable long-reach manipulators are a promising architecture for robotic maintenance of lunar infrastructure such as solar arrays, radiators, and optical surfaces.

</details>


### [187] [IMPASTO: Integrating Model-Based Planning with Learned Dynamics Models for Robotic Oil Painting Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29315)
*Yingke Wang,Hao Li,Yifeng Zhu,Hong-Xing Yu,Ken Goldberg,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Yunzhu Li,Ruohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IMPASTO是一个机器人油画系统，通过自学习像素动态模型和模型预测控制，仅从目标图像序列推断并执行笔触轨迹、力度和颜色，实现油画复制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人复制油画需要控制可变形工具、预测笔触效果并进行多步骤规划，但缺乏人类逐步演示或精确模拟器。研究旨在探索仅凭目标图像序列，机器人能否推断并执行所需的笔触轨迹、力度和颜色。

Method: IMPASTO整合了学习像素动态模型与基于模型的规划：动态模型从图像观察和参数化笔触动作预测画布更新；模型预测控制优化器规划轨迹和力度；力敏感控制器在7自由度机械臂上执行笔触。系统仅通过机器人自学习训练。

Result: IMPASTO在复制精度上优于基线方法，能够近似人类艺术家的单笔触数据集和多笔触艺术作品，展示了从目标图像序列成功推断和执行油画复制的能力。

Conclusion: IMPASTO成功整合了低层力控制、学习动态模型和高层闭环规划，仅通过自学习实现油画复制，为机器人艺术创作提供了新方法。

Abstract: Robotic reproduction of oil paintings using soft brushes and pigments requires force-sensitive control of deformable tools, prediction of brushstroke effects, and multi-step stroke planning, often without human step-by-step demonstrations or faithful simulators. Given only a sequence of target oil painting images, can a robot infer and execute the stroke trajectories, forces, and colors needed to reproduce it? We present IMPASTO, a robotic oil-painting system that integrates learned pixel dynamics models with model-based planning. The dynamics models predict canvas updates from image observations and parameterized stroke actions; a receding-horizon model predictive control optimizer then plans trajectories and forces, while a force-sensitive controller executes strokes on a 7-DoF robot arm. IMPASTO integrates low-level force control, learned dynamics models, and high-level closed-loop planning, learns solely from robot self-play, and approximates human artists' single-stroke datasets and multi-stroke artworks, outperforming baselines in reproduction accuracy. Project website: https://impasto-robopainting.github.io/

</details>


### [188] [Industrial-Grade Robust Robot Vision for Screw Detection and Removal under Uneven Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29363)
*Tomoki Ishikura,Genichiro Matsuda,Takuya Kiyokawa,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自动化拆解空调室外单元的系统，整合两阶段检测方法和基于网格的局部校准策略，在120个真实单元测试中达到78.3%拆解成功率和193秒平均周期时间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日本劳动力减少而废旧家电数量增加，需要自动化回收拆解过程。空调室外单元因尺寸变化、污垢和锈蚀导致自动化拆解困难

Method: 集成任务特定的两阶段检测方法和基于网格的局部校准策略，无需预编程坐标，实现高精度螺丝检测和操作

Result: 螺丝检测召回率达99.8%（即使严重退化），操作精度±0.75mm。在120个真实单元验证中，拆解成功率78.3%，平均周期时间193秒

Conclusion: 该系统证实了工业应用的可行性，能够应对尺寸变化和环境退化问题，为家电回收自动化提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: As the amount of used home appliances is expected to increase despite the decreasing labor force in Japan, there is a need to automate disassembling processes at recycling plants. The automation of disassembling air conditioner outdoor units, however, remains a challenge due to unit size variations and exposure to dirt and rust. To address these challenges, this study proposes an automated system that integrates a task-specific two-stage detection method and a lattice-based local calibration strategy. This approach achieved a screw detection recall of 99.8% despite severe degradation and ensured a manipulation accuracy of +/-0.75 mm without pre-programmed coordinates. In real-world validation with 120 units, the system attained a disassembly success rate of 78.3% and an average cycle time of 193 seconds, confirming its feasibility for industrial application.

</details>


### [189] [Interacting Multiple Model Proprioceptive Odometry for Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29383)
*Wanlei Li,Zichang Chen,Shilei Li,Xiaogang Xiong,Yunjiang Lou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于交互多模型（IMM）的足式机器人本体感知里程计框架，通过多接触假设提升在复杂接触条件下的姿态估计精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人状态估计面临挑战：本体感知里程计可观测性有限，依赖测量约束抑制漂移；当外部传感器不可靠时，主要依赖本体感知测量（特别是接触相关的腿部运动学信息）；现有方法基于理想点接触假设，在实际运动中常被违反，导致约束效果下降和估计精度降低

Method: 提出基于交互多模型（IMM）的本体感知里程计框架，在统一概率框架内整合多个接触假设，实现在线模式切换和概率融合，适应变化的接触条件

Result: 大量仿真和真实世界实验表明，该方法在保持可比计算效率的同时，相比最先进方法实现了更优的姿态估计精度

Conclusion: IMM框架通过处理复杂接触条件，显著提升了足式机器人在外部传感器不可靠时的本体感知里程计性能，为实际运动中的状态估计提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: State estimation for legged robots remains challenging because legged odometry generally suffers from limited observability and therefore depends critically on measurement constraints to suppress drift. When exteroceptive sensors are unreliable or degraded, such constraints are mainly derived from proprioceptive measurements, particularly contact-related leg kinematics information. However, most existing proprioceptive odometry methods rely on an idealized point-contact assumption, which is often violated during real locomotion. Consequently, the effectiveness of proprioceptive constraints may be significantly reduced, resulting in degraded estimation accuracy. To address these limitations, we propose an interacting multiple model (IMM)-based proprioceptive odometry framework for legged robots. By incorporating multiple contact hypotheses within a unified probabilistic framework, the proposed method enables online mode switching and probabilistic fusion under varying contact conditions. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior pose estimation accuracy over state-of-the-art methods while maintaining comparable computational efficiency.

</details>


### [190] [Learning Semantic Priorities for Autonomous Target Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29391)
*Max Lodel,Nils Wilde,Robert Babuška,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种利用专家输入训练语义优先级模型的目标搜索方法，通过组合优化在边界探索规划器中使用学习到的优先级，实现基于语义特征的高效目标搜索


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前目标搜索方法依赖相似领域的大数据集训练，限制了在多样化环境中的适应性。人类专家拥有关于语义关系的高层知识，可以有效指导机器人在多样化且未见过的环境中进行目标搜索
